<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Parse a unified diff (`git diff` output, one file) into rows with old/new</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * line numbers. File headers (`diff --git`, `---`, `+++`, `index`) are dropped.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> parseUnifiedDiff</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">diff</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> FileDiffLine</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">[] {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> oldNo </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> newNo </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> hunk</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">\d</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">)(?:,</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">\d</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> @@(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">$</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">exec</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(raw)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (hunk) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      oldNo </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> Number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(hunk[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">])</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      newNo </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> Number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(hunk[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">])</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      inHunk </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> true</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      rows.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">push</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">({ type: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"hunk"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, content: raw })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">      continue</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">inHunk) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">continue</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (raw.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">startsWith</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">\\</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">continue</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> // "\ No newline at end of file"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> sign</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> raw[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> content</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> raw.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">slice</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (sign </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "+"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) rows.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">push</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">({ type: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"added"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, content, newLine: newNo</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">++</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    else</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (sign </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "-"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) rows.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">push</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">({ type: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"removed"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, content, oldLine: oldNo</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">++</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    else</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (sign </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> " "</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> ||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> raw </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> ""</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) rows.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">push</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">({ type: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"context"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, content, oldLine: oldNo</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">++</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, newLine: newNo</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">++</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  // A trailing empty context row comes from the diff's final newline.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> last</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> rows[rows.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (last </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> last.type </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "context"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> &#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> last.content </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> ""</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> &#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> diff.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">endsWith</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">\n</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)) rows.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">pop</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> rows</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Turn rows back into a patch the diff renderer can parse. Hunk headers are</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * recounted from the rows that are actually there, so a half-streamed hunk</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * still parses cleanly.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> rowsToPatch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">rows</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> FileDiffLine</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">[], </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">filename</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> out</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">`--- a/${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">filename</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">`+++ b/${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">filename</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  while</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> rows.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> header </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> rows[i].type </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "hunk"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> ?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> rows[i].content </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "@@ -1 +1 @@"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (rows[i].type </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "hunk"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">++</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> body</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> FileDiffLine</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">[] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> []</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    while</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> rows.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> &#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> rows[i].type </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!==</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "hunk"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) body.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">push</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(rows[i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">++</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">])</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (body.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> ===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">continue</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> m</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> /</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">^</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">@@ -(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">\d</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">)(?:,</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">\d</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#7EE787;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold"> \+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">\d</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">)(?:,</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">\d</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> @@(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">$</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">exec</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(header)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> first</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> body[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> oldStart</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> m </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> Number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(m[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">]) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (first.oldLine </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">??</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> newStart</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> m </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> Number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(m[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">]) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (first.newLine </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">??</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> oldCount</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> body.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">filter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">r</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> r.type </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!==</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "added"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">length</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> newCount</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> body.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">filter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">r</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> r.type </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!==</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "removed"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">length</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    header </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> `@@ -${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">oldStart</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">},${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">oldCount</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">} +${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">newStart</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">},${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">newCount</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">} @@${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">m</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">?.[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">??</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> ""}`</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    out.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">push</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(header)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> r</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> of</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> body) out.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">push</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((r.type </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "added"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> ?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "+"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> :</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> r.type </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "removed"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> ?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "-"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> :</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> " "</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> r.content)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> out.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">join</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">\n</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">\n</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/*  Lazy diff renderer (highlighter + diff engine stay out of the first bundle) */</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> DiffModules</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> loadDiffRenderer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">() {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  diffPromise </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">??=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">all</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">([</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"@pierre/diffs"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"@pierre/diffs/react"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)]).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(([</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">core</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">react</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">]) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    diffModules </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { core, react }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> diffModules</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> diffPromise</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/** The renderer modules, or `null` on the server and until the chunk loads. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> useDiffRenderer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">() {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">mods</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">setMods</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> React.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">useState</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">DiffModules</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>(diffModules)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  React.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">useEffect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (mods) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">return</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> alive </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> true</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">    loadDiffRenderer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">().</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">m</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> alive </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> setMods</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(m))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      alive </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> false</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  }, [mods])</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> mods</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/** Follow the docs/theme convention: dark mode is `html.dark`. */</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/* Paint the code surface with our `--code` token instead of the highlighter theme bg. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> UNSAFE_CSS</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> /* css */</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> `</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">:host, [data-diffs] { --diffs-light-bg: var(--code); --diffs-dark-bg: var(--code); }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">pre { background-color: var(--code); }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">[data-separator] { font-size: 11px; }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">[data-separator=metadata] { height: 24px; font-family: var(--diffs-font-family); }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">[data-separator=line-info-basic] { height: 26px; }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">`</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/*</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * While streaming, the renderer rebuilds its rows on every update, so the</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * last row of each column is always the newest one: flash it in.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> STREAMING_CSS</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> /* css */</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> `</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">@keyframes file-diff-row-in {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">  from { opacity: 0; translate: -6px 0; box-shadow: inset 100vw 0 color-mix(in oklch, var(--primary) 14%, transparent); }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">[data-content] > [data-line]:last-child { animation: file-diff-row-in 0.45s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1); }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">`</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/** Five-block diffstat ratio of additions to deletions. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> DiffStat</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">additions</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">deletions</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> }</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">additions</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">deletions</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> }) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> total</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> additions </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> deletions</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> add</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> total </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> ?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> :</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Math.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">round</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((additions </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> total) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> del</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> total </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> ?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> :</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Math.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">min</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> add, Math.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">round</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((deletions </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> total) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">span</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> aria-hidden</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> className</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"hidden gap-0.5 sm:flex"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">      {</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">Array.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">({ length: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> }, (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">_</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">        &#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#116329;--shiki-dark:#7EE787">span</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">          key</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">          className</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">={</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">cn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">            "size-1.5 rounded-[calc(var(--radius)*0.2)] transition-colors duration-300"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">            i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> add </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "bg-success"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> :</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> add </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> del </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "bg-destructive"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> :</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "bg-muted shadow-well"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">          )</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> FileDiffProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">A</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> undefined</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> Omit</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ComponentProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"div"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"children"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** File path shown in the header. The directory part is dimmed. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  filename</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Unified diff text. Ignored when `lines`, `fileDiff` or `oldFile`/`newFile` are given. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  diff</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Pre-parsed rows, e.g. appended one by one while an agent streams. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  lines</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> FileDiffLine</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">[]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Old file contents for a two-file diff (`null` = file added). Unchanged regions become expandable. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  oldFile</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** New file contents for a two-file diff (`null` = file deleted). */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  newFile</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Pre-built diff metadata, e.g. after `diffAcceptRejectHunk`. Wins over every other source. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  fileDiff</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> FileDiffMetadata</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Syntax language id, inferred from `filename` when omitted. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  language</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** `streaming` shows a spinner and follows the end of the diff. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  status</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "streaming"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "complete"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  open</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> boolean</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  defaultOpen</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> boolean</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">  onOpenChange</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">open</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> boolean</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> void</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Fold the diff away when status flips from streaming to complete. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  collapseOnComplete</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> boolean</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Max height of the rows area in px. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  maxHeight</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> number</span></span>
